## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)

## 约束

- Qwen2.5 系列代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，已验证不可用
- 根分区 ~30GB，模型/数据必须在 `/root/autodl-tmp/`
- OLMo 必须用 `olmo-1b-hf` 目录（不是 `olmo-1b`）
- DCOL margin_lr 不能超过 1e-4（1e-3 导致 embedding collapse）
- OLMo cron 监控有 false failure 风险（子进程 PID vs pipeline 主进程）
- DeBERTa disentangled attention 与 contrastive loss 数值不兼容

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

Prior z-score recovery (0.13→0.68) was with LR, not RF.\"}, \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Z-score standardization has negligible effect on RF cross-strategy LOO. temp09 d1v2 AUC remains inverted: GPT-2 XL 0.1288→0.1348, OLMo 0.2529→0.2497, Pythia 0.576→0.594. Root cause: RF is a tree-based model invariant to monotonic feature transforms — z-score shifts/scales features but preserves relative ordering, so split thresholds adapt automatically. Prior independent verification (GPT-2 XL 0.13→0.68, OLMo 0.25→0.83) was with a scale-sensitive classifier (LR), not RF. Raw results match cross_strategy_loo_001 exactly (mean acc=0.5908). Non-temp09 impact negligible (mean Δd1v2=+0.006). Conclusion: covariate shift hypothesis is correct but the z-score fix is classifier-dependent — works for LR, not for RF.\"}, \"cross_model_loo_v1\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"notes\": \"## Comparison: temp_only τ=0.9 vs baseline nucleus p=0.95\\n\\n### 4-class classification (d0-d3)\\n| Metric | Baseline (nucleus p=0.95) | Temp09 (τ=0.9) | Δ | -0.0007 |\\n| AUC 1v2 | 0.8607 | 0.8327 | -0.028 |\\n| AUC 2v3 | 0.8005 | 0.7928 | -0.008 |\\n\\n### 3-class classification (d1-d3 only)\\n| Metric | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|--------|----------|--------|---|\\n| Full acc | 70.8% | 69.83% | -1.0pp |\\n| Filtered acc | — | 72.52% [70.6, 74.3] | — |\\n| Filtered d1v2 AUC | — | 0.8656 | — |\\n| Filtered d2v3 AUC | — | 0.8295 | — |\\n\\n### Feature importance (top-5, same ranking)\\n1. var_surprisal (0.178 vs 0.169)\\n2. p90_ppl (0.150 vs 0.158)\\n3. mean_ppl (0.114 vs 0.120)\\n4. mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. Hypothesis confirmed: temperature-only decoding preserves recursive depth signal.\"}, \"mvp_pipeline_pythia_002\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T12:07:25.860Z\", \"updated_at\": -8.6pp |\\\\n| 3-class acc | 59.2% | 70.8% | -11.6pp |\\\\n| d0v1 AUC | 0.9927 | 0.9975 | -0.005 |\\\\n| d1v2 AUC | 0.7518 | 0.8607 | -0.109 |\\\\n| d2v3 AUC | 0.6918 | 0.8005 | -0.109 |\\\\n\\\\n### vs temp09 对比\\\\n| Metric | topk50 | temp09 | delta |\\\\n|--------|--------|--------|-------|\\\\n| 4-class acc | 69.6% | 76.25% | -6.65pp |\\\\n| 3-class acc | 59.2% | 69.83% | -10.6pp |\\\\n| d1v2 p75_ppl\\\\n\\\\n### Code contamination (narrow)\\\\nd0=0.4%, d1=7.7%, d2=12.3%, d3=13.1%（高于 baseline，top-k 产生更多 code-like text）\\\\nFiltered 3-class 性能反而略高于 unfiltered (61.3% vs 59.2%)，说明 code contamination 不是性能的主要驱动\\\\n\\\\n### 结论\\\\nSUCCESS — 深度信号在 top-k 采样下仍然存在（69.6% >> 25% chance），但信号强度显著低于 nucleus (-8.6pp) 和 temp09 (-6.65pp)。\\\\n跨策略泛化成立（信号存在），但不同采样策略对信号强度有影响。Top-k 的截断式采样比连续概率重分配（nucleus/temperature）保留的深度信号更弱。\\\"}, \\\"cross_model_gpt2xl_001\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T14:10:51.209Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. inversion in RF — RF is scale-invariant (tree splits unaffected by monotonic transforms). Prior z-score recovery (0.13→0.68) was with LR, not RF.\"}, \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Z-score standardization has negligible effect on RF cross-strategy LOO. temp09 d1v2 AUC remains inverted: GPT-2 XL 0.1288→0.1348, OLMo 0.2529→0.2497, Pythia 0.576→0.594. Root cause: RF is a tree-based model invariant to monotonic feature transforms — z-score shifts/scales features but preserves relative ordering, so split thresholds adapt automatically. Prior independent verification (GPT-2 XL 0.13→0.68, OLMo 0.25→0.83) was with a scale-sensitive classifier (LR), not RF. Raw results match cross_strategy_loo_001 exactly (mean acc=0.5908). Non-temp09 impact negligible (mean Δd1v2=+0.006). Conclusion: covariate shift hypothesis is correct but the z-score fix is classifier-dependent — works for LR, not for RF.\"}, \"cross_model_loo_v1\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": 0.8295 | — |\\n\\n### Feature importance (top-5, same ranking)\\n1. var_surprisal (0.178 vs 0.169)\\n2. p90_ppl (0.150 vs 0.158)\\n3. mean_ppl (0.114 vs 0.120)\\n4. mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold.
